### 词群 11.01｜一种风格怎样成为潮流 {#group-11-01}

**串联方式：** 从审美、奢华和风格进入趋势、流行与盛行，再看模特、偶像的带动。

<summary><h4 id="word-11-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">perfect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-001" aria-label="定位 perfect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-001" data-mark-label="perfect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="perfect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="perfect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 perfect" title="在有道查询 perfect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 完美的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her extravagant style made the dress <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perfect</strong></a> for a fashion show.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她奢华的风格使这条裙子非常适合时装秀。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perfect</strong></a> sense of style does not require extravagant spending on fashion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">出色的时尚品味并不需要在服饰上奢侈消费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fashion editor found a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perfect</strong></a> example of extravagant style in the designer’s new collection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">时尚编辑在设计师的新系列中找到了奢华风格的完美范例。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">trend<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-005" aria-label="定位 trend">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-005" data-mark-label="trend" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trend：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trend：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trend" title="在有道查询 trend">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.趋势，倾向</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The current <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trend</strong></a> reflects a tendency towards comfort and the growing popularity of loose clothing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当前趋势反映了追求舒适的倾向，以及宽松服装日益流行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The popularity of second-hand shops supports the current <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trend</strong></a> and a tendency to buy less.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">二手店的流行推动了当下潮流，以及减少购买的倾向。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Designers study the current <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trend</strong></a>, the popularity of new fabrics and consumers’ tendency to follow celebrities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师研究当前趋势、新面料的流行程度以及消费者追随名人的倾向。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fashion trend</span> · 时尚趋势</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">demand</a></span> · 需求</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-006">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-006" class="vocabulary-word-title">tendency<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-006" aria-label="定位 tendency">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-006" data-mark-label="tendency" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tendency：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tendency：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tendency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tendency" title="在有道查询 tendency">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-006</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The current trend reflects a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tendency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tendency</strong></a> towards comfort and the growing popularity of loose clothing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当前趋势反映了追求舒适的倾向，以及宽松服装日益流行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The popularity of second-hand shops supports the current trend and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tendency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tendency</strong></a> to buy less.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">二手店的流行推动了当下潮流，以及减少购买的倾向。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Designers study the current trend, the popularity of new fabrics and consumers’ <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tendency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tendency</strong></a> to follow celebrities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师研究当前趋势、新面料的流行程度以及消费者追随名人的倾向。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">popularity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-008" aria-label="定位 popularity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-008" data-mark-label="popularity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="popularity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="popularity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=popularity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 popularity" title="在有道查询 popularity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 流行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The current trend reflects a tendency towards comfort and the growing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=popularity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>popularity</strong></a> of loose clothing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当前趋势反映了追求舒适的倾向，以及宽松服装日益流行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=popularity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>popularity</strong></a> of second-hand shops supports the current trend and a tendency to buy less.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">二手店的流行推动了当下潮流，以及减少购买的倾向。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Designers study the current trend, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=popularity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>popularity</strong></a> of new fabrics and consumers’ tendency to follow celebrities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师研究当前趋势、新面料的流行程度以及消费者追随名人的倾向。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gain popularity</span> · 变得流行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">demand</a></span> · 需求</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">vogue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-009" aria-label="定位 vogue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-009" data-mark-label="vogue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vogue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vogue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vogue" title="在有道查询 vogue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.流行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model became a style icon and teenage idol, helping bold colours come into <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vogue</strong></a> and prevail for several seasons.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位模特成为风格偶像和青少年偶像，推动大胆色彩流行并持续盛行了好几季。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A fashion icon or pop idol may set a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vogue</strong></a>, but a practical model of dress is more likely to prevail.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">时尚偶像或流行偶像可能引领潮流，但实用的着装模式更可能长久盛行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The model hopes simple clothes will prevail, even though her fans treat her as an icon and idol of whatever is in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vogue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位模特希望简单衣着能盛行，尽管粉丝把她当成流行潮流的风格偶像和崇拜对象。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in vogue</span> · 流行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">influence</a></span> · 影响力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebrity%20endorsement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebrity endorsement</a></span> · 名人代言</li></ul>

### 词群 11.02｜装饰如何改变外观 {#group-11-02}

**串联方式：** 珠宝与装饰动作连接到刺绣、美发和染色，再观察面纱、服装与化装场景。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">luxury<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-014" aria-label="定位 luxury">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-014" data-mark-label="luxury" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="luxury：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="luxury：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luxury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 luxury" title="在有道查询 luxury">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 奢侈；奢侈品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luxury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>luxury</strong></a> jewellery shop displays a jade pendant beside a rare gem and a jewel-encrusted ring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">奢华珠宝店在玉吊坠旁陈列一颗稀有宝石和一枚镶满宝石的戒指。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A jade jewel can be the centrepiece of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=luxury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>luxury</strong></a> jewellery even when it is less costly than another gem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使玉石比另一种宝石便宜，它仍可以成为奢华首饰的核心。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">adorn<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-019" aria-label="定位 adorn">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-019" data-mark-label="adorn" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="adorn：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="adorn：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adorn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 adorn" title="在有道查询 adorn">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.装饰，装扮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They will embroider flowers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adorn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adorn</strong></a> the dress, then embellish the collar with a small ornament.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们会绣花装饰裙子，再用小饰件装点衣领。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use thread to embroider the border, beads to embellish the sleeves and one ornament to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adorn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adorn</strong></a> the belt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用线绣边、用珠子装饰袖子，再用一件饰物点缀腰带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than add a heavy ornament, the designer will embroider a light pattern to embellish and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adorn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adorn</strong></a> the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师将绣一个轻巧图案装饰布料，而不是加上沉重饰件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">adorn a dress</span> · 装饰裙子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embroidery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embroidery</a></span> · 刺绣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decoration</a></span> · 装饰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-020">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.装饰晶；v.装饰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They will embroider flowers to adorn the dress, then embellish the collar with a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ornament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ornament</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们会绣花装饰裙子，再用小饰件装点衣领。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use thread to embroider the border, beads to embellish the sleeves and one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ornament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ornament</strong></a> to adorn the belt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用线绣边、用珠子装饰袖子，再用一件饰物点缀腰带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than add a heavy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ornament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ornament</strong></a>, the designer will embroider a light pattern to embellish and adorn the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师将绣一个轻巧图案装饰布料，而不是加上沉重饰件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gold ornament</span> · 金饰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embroidery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embroidery</a></span> · 刺绣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decoration</a></span> · 装饰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They will embroider flowers to adorn the dress, then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embellish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embellish</strong></a> the collar with a small ornament.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们会绣花装饰裙子，再用小饰件装点衣领。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use thread to embroider the border, beads to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embellish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embellish</strong></a> the sleeves and one ornament to adorn the belt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用线绣边、用珠子装饰袖子，再用一件饰物点缀腰带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than add a heavy ornament, the designer will embroider a light pattern to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embellish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embellish</strong></a> and adorn the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师将绣一个轻巧图案装饰布料，而不是加上沉重饰件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">embellish a collar</span> · 装饰衣领</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embroidery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embroidery</a></span> · 刺绣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decoration</a></span> · 装饰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use thread to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embroider&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embroider</strong></a> the border, beads to embellish the sleeves and one ornament to adorn the belt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用线绣边、用珠子装饰袖子，再用一件饰物点缀腰带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than add a heavy ornament, the designer will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embroider&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embroider</strong></a> a light pattern to embellish and adorn the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师将绣一个轻巧图案装饰布料，而不是加上沉重饰件。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-023">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-023" class="vocabulary-word-title">hairdressing<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-023" aria-label="定位 hairdressing">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-023" data-mark-label="hairdressing" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hairdressing：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hairdressing：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hairdressing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hairdressing" title="在有道查询 hairdressing">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-023</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n美发，理发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hairdressing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hairdressing</strong></a> salon uses a dye containing a plant-based pigment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美发沙龙使用含植物色素的染发剂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hairdressing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hairdressing</strong></a> course explained how dye deposits pigment on hair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的美发课程解释了染发剂如何把色素附着在头发上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hairdressing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hairdressing</strong></a> apprentice tested the dye to see how strongly its pigment would colour a sample.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美发学徒测试染发剂，观察其色素对样品的着色程度。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a hairdressing salon</span> · 美发沙龙</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colouring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">colouring</a></span> · 染色</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stylist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stylist</a></span> · 造型师</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-026">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 化装舞会；v. 化装；假扮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She wore a silver costume and a lace veil to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masquerade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>masquerade</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她穿银色服装、戴蕾丝面纱参加化装舞会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The costume for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masquerade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>masquerade</strong></a> included a long veil and a painted mask.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化装舞会服装包括一条长面纱和一个彩绘面具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masquerade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>masquerade</strong></a>, nobody recognised the guest hidden beneath the costume and veil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">舞会上，没有人认出藏在服装和面纱下的那位客人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a masquerade ball</span> · 化装舞会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mask&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mask</a></span> · 面具</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disguise</a></span> · 伪装</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She wore a silver <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=costume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>costume</strong></a> and a lace veil to the masquerade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她穿银色服装、戴蕾丝面纱参加化装舞会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=costume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>costume</strong></a> for the masquerade included a long veil and a painted mask.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化装舞会服装包括一条长面纱和一个彩绘面具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the masquerade, nobody recognised the guest hidden beneath the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=costume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>costume</strong></a> and veil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">舞会上，没有人认出藏在服装和面纱下的那位客人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a theatrical costume</span> · 戏剧服装</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mask&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mask</a></span> · 面具</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disguise</a></span> · 伪装</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 11.03｜怎样描述外貌与吸引力 {#group-11-03}

**串联方式：** 把吸引人、得体、精致和优雅放到整体外观，再辨妆容及常见外貌评价。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">fascinate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-029" aria-label="定位 fascinate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-029" data-mark-label="fascinate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fascinate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fascinate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fascinate" title="在有道查询 fascinate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.迷住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Exquisite embroidery can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fascinate</strong></a> visitors even when displayed on a simple, decent dress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使展示在简单得体的裙子上，精致刺绣也能吸引访客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fascinate</strong></a> anyone interested in how decent everyday clothes can contain exquisite details.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次展览会吸引所有对得体日常服装中的精致细节感兴趣的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A decent photograph may record the exquisite stitching, but seeing the gown in person can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascinate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fascinate</strong></a> even experienced designers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一张不错的照片可以记录精致针脚，但亲眼看见礼服甚至能吸引经验丰富的设计师。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fascinate an audience</span> · 吸引观众</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=craftsmanship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">craftsmanship</a></span> · 工艺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detail</a></span> · 细节</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.优美，优雅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She walked with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grace</strong></a> and wore the simple gown with effortless elegance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她步态优美，穿着简单礼服，显得从容典雅。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The collection combines the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grace</strong></a> of flowing fabric with the elegance of clean lines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个系列融合了流动面料的优美与简洁线条的典雅。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dancer’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grace</strong></a> gave the plain costume an unexpected elegance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">舞者优美的举止使素戏服呈现出意想不到的典雅。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">move with grace</span> · 优雅移动</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She walked with grace and wore the simple gown with effortless <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elegance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elegance</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她步态优美，穿着简单礼服，显得从容典雅。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The collection combines the grace of flowing fabric with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elegance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elegance</strong></a> of clean lines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个系列融合了流动面料的优美与简洁线条的典雅。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dancer’s grace gave the plain costume an unexpected <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elegance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elegance</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">舞者优美的举止使素戏服呈现出意想不到的典雅。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Cosmetics can change your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appearance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appearance</strong></a>, but heavy make-up is not necessary for every occasion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化妆品能改变外貌，但并非每个场合都需要浓妆。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cosmetics company showed how light make-up could enhance a natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appearance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appearance</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化妆品公司展示了淡妆如何提升自然外貌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appearance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appearance</strong></a> changed after the stylist removed the stage make-up and put away the cosmetics.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">造型师卸掉舞台妆并收起化妆品后，她的外貌发生了变化。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.化妆品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cosmetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Cosmetics</strong></a> can change your appearance, but heavy make-up is not necessary for every occasion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化妆品能改变外貌，但并非每个场合都需要浓妆。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cosmetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cosmetics</strong></a> company showed how light make-up could enhance a natural appearance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">化妆品公司展示了淡妆如何提升自然外貌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her appearance changed after the stylist removed the stage make-up and put away the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cosmetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cosmetics</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">造型师卸掉舞台妆并收起化妆品后，她的外貌发生了变化。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-037">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-037" class="vocabulary-word-title">handsome<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-037" aria-label="定位 handsome">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-037" data-mark-label="handsome" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="handsome：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="handsome：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handsome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 handsome" title="在有道查询 handsome">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-037</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.数量大的；英俊的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handsome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handsome</strong></a> actor gave a charming smile in a beautiful garden beside a pretty cottage and an ugly shed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">英俊演员在美丽花园里露出迷人笑容，旁边是一间漂亮小屋和一座难看的棚屋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pretty costume and charming manner helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handsome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handsome</strong></a> performer create a beautiful scene in an ugly room.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">漂亮戏服和迷人举止帮助英俊表演者在难看房间里营造出美丽场景。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photographer chose a beautiful backdrop for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handsome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handsome</strong></a> model, hiding an ugly wall behind a pretty screen with a charming floral design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">摄影师为英俊模特选了美丽背景，用一块带迷人花卉图案的漂亮屏风遮住难看的墙。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a handsome actor</span> · 英俊的演员</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity helps clothe children with a school uniform, a warm garment and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dress</strong></a> for special occasions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慈善机构为儿童提供校服、保暖衣物和特殊场合穿的连衣裙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To clothe the cast, we need one uniform, a period <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dress</strong></a> and a plain garment for each extra.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">给演员配衣时，我们需要一套制服、一条年代连衣裙，以及每位群演一件素色衣服。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fund will clothe students who cannot afford a uniform or another suitable garment, including a simple <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dress</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">基金将为买不起制服或其他合适衣物的学生提供衣物，其中包括简单连衣裙。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a summer dress</span> · 夏季连衣裙</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clothe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clothe</strong></a> the cast, we need one uniform, a period dress and a plain garment for each extra.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">给演员配衣时，我们需要一套制服、一条年代连衣裙，以及每位群演一件素色衣服。</p></li>

</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-046">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.洗衣店；要(或正在)洗的衣物，刚洗好的衣物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the clean overall in the wardrobe and send the overcoat to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laundry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laundry</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把干净罩衣放进衣柜，把大衣送去洗衣店。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His wardrobe contained a work overall and an overcoat, both fresh from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laundry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laundry</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的衣柜里有一件工作罩衣和一件大衣，都是刚洗好的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before doing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laundry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laundry</strong></a>, she removed a stained overall from the wardrobe and checked the overcoat’s care label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洗衣前，她从衣柜拿出有污渍的罩衣，并查看大衣的护理标签。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">do the laundry</span> · 洗衣服</p>

</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the clean overall in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wardrobe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wardrobe</strong></a> and send the overcoat to the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把干净罩衣放进衣柜，把大衣送去洗衣店。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wardrobe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wardrobe</strong></a> contained a work overall and an overcoat, both fresh from the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的衣柜里有一件工作罩衣和一件大衣，都是刚洗好的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before doing the laundry, she removed a stained overall from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wardrobe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wardrobe</strong></a> and checked the overcoat’s care label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洗衣前，她从衣柜拿出有污渍的罩衣，并查看大衣的护理标签。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the clean overall in the wardrobe and send the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overcoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overcoat</strong></a> to the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把干净罩衣放进衣柜，把大衣送去洗衣店。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His wardrobe contained a work overall and an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overcoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overcoat</strong></a>, both fresh from the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的衣柜里有一件工作罩衣和一件大衣，都是刚洗好的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before doing the laundry, she removed a stained overall from the wardrobe and checked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overcoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overcoat</strong></a>’s care label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洗衣前，她从衣柜拿出有污渍的罩衣，并查看大衣的护理标签。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shop pairs a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sweater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sweater</strong></a> and jacket with a skirt, jeans or tailored trousers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">商店把毛衣和夹克搭配裙子、牛仔裤或剪裁合身的长裤。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pack a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sweater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sweater</strong></a>, a jacket and jeans, plus a skirt or smart trousers for dinner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带上毛衣、夹克和牛仔裤，再带条裙子或整洁长裤用于晚餐。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. ［尤英］裤子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shop pairs a sweater and jacket with a skirt, jeans or tailored <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trousers&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trousers</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">商店把毛衣和夹克搭配裙子、牛仔裤或剪裁合身的长裤。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pack a sweater, a jacket and jeans, plus a skirt or smart <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trousers&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trousers</strong></a> for dinner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带上毛衣、夹克和牛仔裤，再带条裙子或整洁长裤用于晚餐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She tried the jacket over a sweater, comparing how it looked with jeans, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trousers&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trousers</strong></a> and a skirt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把夹克套在毛衣外，比较它与牛仔裤、长裤及裙子的搭配效果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">smart trousers</span> · 整洁正式的裤子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=layering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">layering</a></span> · 叠穿</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=casual%20wear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">casual wear</a></span> · 休闲服</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">vest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-069" aria-label="定位 vest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-069" data-mark-label="vest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vest" title="在有道查询 vest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. ［英］汗衫；［美］西装背心</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He wore a cotton <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vest</strong></a> beneath his shirt to keep warm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他在衬衫下穿棉汗衫保暖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The runner chose a lightweight <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vest</strong></a> for the summer race.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跑者为夏季比赛选择了轻薄背心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In American English, a suit <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vest</strong></a> is the garment British speakers usually call a waistcoat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美式英语中的西装背心 vest 通常对应英式英语的 waistcoat。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cotton vest</span> · 棉汗衫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waistcoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">waistcoat</a></span> · 西装背心</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undershirt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">undershirt</a></span> · 内穿汗衫</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-070">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The costume rail held a monk’s robe, an evening gown and a hooded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cloak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cloak</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">戏服架上有僧袍、晚礼服和连帽斗篷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She wore a robe over her gown backstage and added a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cloak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cloak</strong></a> for the outdoor scene.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她在后台把长袍罩在礼服外，演室外场景时又加了斗篷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum displayed a ceremonial robe beside a silk gown and a winter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cloak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cloak</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆在一件丝绸礼服和冬季斗篷旁展出了一件礼仪长袍。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a hooded cloak</span> · 连帽斗篷</p>

### 词群 11.05｜衣服细节与随身配件 {#group-11-05}

**串联方式：** 看清扣子、衣领、袖子和帽檐，再搭配手套、围巾、钱包、袜子和鞋靴。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">clasp<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-057" aria-label="定位 clasp">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-057" data-mark-label="clasp" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="clasp：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="clasp：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clasp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 clasp" title="在有道查询 clasp">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.搭扣；v.扣住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fasten the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clasp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clasp</strong></a> on the chain, close the top button and wrap the scarf loosely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扣好链子的搭扣，扣上最上面的纽扣，再松松围好围巾。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chain caught on a button as she tried to wrap the shawl around her shoulders and close its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clasp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clasp</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把披肩裹上肩并扣好搭扣时，链子勾住了纽扣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clasp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clasp</strong></a> and chain before you button the coat and wrap yourself in a scarf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扣大衣纽扣、围围巾前，先检查搭扣和链子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a necklace clasp</span> · 项链扣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fastening&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fastening</a></span> · 扣合装置</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accessory</a></span> · 配饰</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-058">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fasten the clasp on the chain, close the top <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=button&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>button</strong></a> and wrap the scarf loosely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扣好链子的搭扣，扣上最上面的纽扣，再松松围好围巾。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chain caught on a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=button&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>button</strong></a> as she tried to wrap the shawl around her shoulders and close its clasp.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把披肩裹上肩并扣好搭扣时，链子勾住了纽扣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check the clasp and chain before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=button&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>button</strong></a> the coat and wrap yourself in a scarf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扣大衣纽扣、围围巾前，先检查搭扣和链子。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She packed a scarf and handkerchief in her bag, with coins in a purse and cards in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wallet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wallet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把围巾和手帕装进包里，硬币放零钱包，卡放皮夹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lost-property box contained a scarf, a handkerchief, a purse and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wallet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wallet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">失物招领箱里有围巾、手帕、零钱包和钱包。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Wrap the purse and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wallet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wallet</strong></a> in the scarf, but keep the handkerchief in your pocket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用围巾包好零钱包和钱包，但把手帕放在口袋里。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a leather wallet</span> · 皮钱包</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belongings&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">belongings</a></span> · 随身物品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pocket&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pocket</a></span> · 口袋</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stain on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collar</strong></a> matched the mark on the left sleeve.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">衣领上的污渍与左袖上的痕迹相同。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tailor shortened the sleeve and adjusted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collar</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁缝改短了袖子，调整了衣领。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-073">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sock</strong></a> and a stocking lay beside a slipper, a boot and a loose lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短袜和长筒袜放在一只拖鞋、一只靴子和一根散鞋带旁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She put a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sock</strong></a> inside the boot, folded a stocking over the slipper and tied the spare lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把短袜塞进靴子，把长筒袜叠在拖鞋上，再系好备用鞋带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The costume box contained a striped <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sock</strong></a>, a silk stocking, a soft slipper and a boot with a broken lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">戏服箱里有条纹短袜、丝袜、软拖鞋和一只鞋带断了的靴子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wool sock</span> · 羊毛袜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=footwear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">footwear</a></span> · 鞋类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hosiery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hosiery</a></span> · 袜类</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sock and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stocking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stocking</strong></a> lay beside a slipper, a boot and a loose lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短袜和长筒袜放在一只拖鞋、一只靴子和一根散鞋带旁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She put a sock inside the boot, folded a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stocking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stocking</strong></a> over the slipper and tied the spare lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把短袜塞进靴子，把长筒袜叠在拖鞋上，再系好备用鞋带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The costume box contained a striped sock, a silk <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stocking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stocking</strong></a>, a soft slipper and a boot with a broken lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">戏服箱里有条纹短袜、丝袜、软拖鞋和一只鞋带断了的靴子。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She put a sock inside the boot, folded a stocking over the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slipper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slipper</strong></a> and tied the spare lace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把短袜塞进靴子，把长筒袜叠在拖鞋上，再系好备用鞋带。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sock and a stocking lay beside a slipper, a boot and a loose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lace</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短袜和长筒袜放在一只拖鞋、一只靴子和一根散鞋带旁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She put a sock inside the boot, folded a stocking over the slipper and tied the spare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lace</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把短袜塞进靴子，把长筒袜叠在拖鞋上，再系好备用鞋带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The costume box contained a striped sock, a silk stocking, a soft slipper and a boot with a broken <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lace</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">戏服箱里有条纹短袜、丝袜、软拖鞋和一只鞋带断了的靴子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a shoe lace</span> · 鞋带</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=footwear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">footwear</a></span> · 鞋类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hosiery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hosiery</a></span> · 袜类</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 11.06｜衣服怎样缝制 {#group-11-06}

**串联方式：** 由裁缝、纺线和针脚进入针线，再认带子、条纹、丝带和腰带；stripe 是图案。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">tailor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-078" aria-label="定位 tailor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-078" data-mark-label="tailor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tailor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tailor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tailor" title="在有道查询 tailor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.裁缝；v.专门制作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tailor</strong></a> can spin wool into thread, thread a needle and sew with a neat stitch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁缝能把羊毛纺成线、穿针，并缝出整齐针脚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sew, ask the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tailor</strong></a> which needle and stitch to use with the yarn you spin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缝制前，问裁缝用你纺的线该配什么针和针法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop taught us to spin yarn and use a needle to sew a stitch under the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tailor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tailor</strong></a>’s guidance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教我们纺线，并在裁缝指导下用针缝针脚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a skilled tailor</span> · 熟练裁缝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">seam</a></span> · 接缝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewing%20machine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sewing machine</a></span> · 缝纫机</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">sew<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-079" aria-label="定位 sew">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-079" data-mark-label="sew" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sew：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sew：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sew" title="在有道查询 sew">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.缝制，缝补</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tailor can spin wool into thread, thread a needle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sew</strong></a> with a neat stitch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁缝能把羊毛纺成线、穿针，并缝出整齐针脚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sew</strong></a>, ask the tailor which needle and stitch to use with the yarn you spin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缝制前，问裁缝用你纺的线该配什么针和针法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop taught us to spin yarn and use a needle to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sew</strong></a> a stitch under the tailor’s guidance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教我们纺线，并在裁缝指导下用针缝针脚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sew a seam</span> · 缝接缝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">seam</a></span> · 接缝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewing%20machine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sewing machine</a></span> · 缝纫机</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">spin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-080" aria-label="定位 spin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-080" data-mark-label="spin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spin" title="在有道查询 spin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-080</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.纺；(使)旋转；n.高速旋转；(乘车)兜风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tailor can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spin</strong></a> wool into thread, thread a needle and sew with a neat stitch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁缝能把羊毛纺成线、穿针，并缝出整齐针脚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sew, ask the tailor which needle and stitch to use with the yarn you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spin</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缝制前，问裁缝用你纺的线该配什么针和针法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop taught us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spin</strong></a> yarn and use a needle to sew a stitch under the tailor’s guidance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教我们纺线，并在裁缝指导下用针缝针脚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spin wool</span> · 纺羊毛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">seam</a></span> · 接缝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewing%20machine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sewing machine</a></span> · 缝纫机</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sew, ask the tailor which needle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stitch</strong></a> to use with the yarn you spin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缝制前，问裁缝用你纺的线该配什么针和针法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop taught us to spin yarn and use a needle to sew a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stitch</strong></a> under the tailor’s guidance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教我们纺线，并在裁缝指导下用针缝针脚。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">pin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-083" aria-label="定位 pin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-083" data-mark-label="pin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pin" title="在有道查询 pin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 别针，大头针；v.别住，钉住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pin</strong></a> to hold the strap, sew it with strong thread and remove the temporary string.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用别针固定肩带，用结实线缝牢，再拆掉临时细绳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The repair kit contains a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pin</strong></a>, thread and string for fixing a loose strap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修理包里有别针、线和细绳，用来修松动带子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She replaced the string around the strap with thread and secured the end with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pin</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她用缝线替换带子上的细绳，并用别针固定线头。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a safety pin</span> · 安全别针</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attachment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attachment</a></span> · 固定装置</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reinforcement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reinforcement</a></span> · 加固</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-084">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use a pin to hold the strap, sew it with strong thread and remove the temporary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=string&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>string</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用别针固定肩带，用结实线缝牢，再拆掉临时细绳。</p></li>

### 词群 11.07｜首饰、面料与穿着状态 {#group-11-07}

**串联方式：** 从手链、项链和珠子切换到纺织品、绒布与羊毛，再描述补丁、破旧和松紧。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">bracelet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-090" aria-label="定位 bracelet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-090" data-mark-label="bracelet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bracelet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bracelet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bracelet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bracelet" title="在有道查询 bracelet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-090</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bracelet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bracelet</strong></a> and necklace each contain a blue glass bead.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手镯和项链上各有一颗蓝玻璃珠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A bead fell from the necklace as she reached for her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bracelet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bracelet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她伸手拿手镯时，一颗珠子从项链上掉下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The craft kit has enough bead supplies to make a necklace and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bracelet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bracelet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手工套装里的珠子材料足够做一条项链和一只手链。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bracelet and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necklace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necklace</strong></a> each contain a blue glass bead.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手镯和项链上各有一颗蓝玻璃珠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A bead fell from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necklace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necklace</strong></a> as she reached for her bracelet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她伸手拿手镯时，一颗珠子从项链上掉下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The craft kit has enough bead supplies to make a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necklace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necklace</strong></a> and a bracelet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手工套装里的珠子材料足够做一条项链和一只手链。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a pearl necklace</span> · 珍珠项链</p>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-092">
