<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bracelet and necklace each contain a blue glass <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bead</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手镯和项链上各有一颗蓝玻璃珠。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bead</strong></a> fell from the necklace as she reached for her bracelet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她伸手拿手镯时，一颗珠子从项链上掉下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The craft kit has enough <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bead</strong></a> supplies to make a necklace and a bracelet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">手工套装里的珠子材料足够做一条项链和一只手链。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-093">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-093" class="vocabulary-word-title">textile<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-093" aria-label="定位 textile">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-093" data-mark-label="textile" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="textile：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="textile：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=textile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 textile" title="在有道查询 textile">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-093</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.纺织的；n.纺织品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=textile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>textile</strong></a> exhibition compares velvet made from silk with cloth woven from wool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纺织品展览比较了丝制天鹅绒与羊毛织物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=textile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>textile</strong></a> mill produces wool fabric and a cotton velvet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家纺织厂生产羊毛布料和棉天鹅绒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=textile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>textile</strong></a> design examined the surface of velvet and the warmth of wool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纺织设计专业学生考察了天鹅绒的表面和羊毛的保暖性。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the textile industry</span> · 纺织业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pile</a></span> · 绒毛</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weaving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">weaving</a></span> · 织造</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The textile mill produces wool fabric and a cotton <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velvet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>velvet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家纺织厂生产羊毛布料和棉天鹅绒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of textile design examined the surface of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=velvet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>velvet</strong></a> and the warmth of wool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纺织设计专业学生考察了天鹅绒的表面和羊毛的保暖性。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">patch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-096" aria-label="定位 patch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-096" data-mark-label="patch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="patch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="patch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 patch" title="在有道查询 patch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.补丁；(与周围不同的)小片；v.补缀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shabby jacket was too tight, so she cut a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patch</strong></a> from it and used the rest as a rag.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这件破旧夹克太紧，她就从上面剪出一块补丁，其余当抹布。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tight stitch held the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patch</strong></a> on the shabby coat, while a spare rag covered the workbench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧密针脚把补丁固定在破旧外套上，另一块破布盖着工作台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than turn the shabby trousers into a rag, he added a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patch</strong></a> and loosened the tight waistband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他没有把破旧裤子改成抹布，而是补上补丁、放松了紧腰头。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sew on a patch</span> · 缝补丁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修补</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wear%20and%20tear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wear and tear</a></span> · 磨损</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">rag<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-097" aria-label="定位 rag">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-097" data-mark-label="rag" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rag：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rag：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rag" title="在有道查询 rag">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.破布</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shabby jacket was too tight, so she cut a patch from it and used the rest as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rag</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这件破旧夹克太紧，她就从上面剪出一块补丁，其余当抹布。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tight stitch held the patch on the shabby coat, while a spare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rag</strong></a> covered the workbench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧密针脚把补丁固定在破旧外套上，另一块破布盖着工作台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than turn the shabby trousers into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rag</strong></a>, he added a patch and loosened the tight waistband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他没有把破旧裤子改成抹布，而是补上补丁、放松了紧腰头。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cleaning rag</span> · 清洁抹布</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修补</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wear%20and%20tear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wear and tear</a></span> · 磨损</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">shabby<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-098" aria-label="定位 shabby">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-098" data-mark-label="shabby" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shabby：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shabby：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shabby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shabby" title="在有道查询 shabby">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.破旧的；衣衫褴褛的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shabby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shabby</strong></a> jacket was too tight, so she cut a patch from it and used the rest as a rag.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这件破旧夹克太紧，她就从上面剪出一块补丁，其余当抹布。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tight stitch held the patch on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shabby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shabby</strong></a> coat, while a spare rag covered the workbench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧密针脚把补丁固定在破旧外套上，另一块破布盖着工作台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than turn the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shabby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shabby</strong></a> trousers into a rag, he added a patch and loosened the tight waistband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他没有把破旧裤子改成抹布，而是补上补丁、放松了紧腰头。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a shabby coat</span> · 破旧外套</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">tight<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-099" aria-label="定位 tight">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-099" data-mark-label="tight" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tight：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tight：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tight" title="在有道查询 tight">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.紧的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shabby jacket was too <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tight</strong></a>, so she cut a patch from it and used the rest as a rag.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这件破旧夹克太紧，她就从上面剪出一块补丁，其余当抹布。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tight</strong></a> stitch held the patch on the shabby coat, while a spare rag covered the workbench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">紧密针脚把补丁固定在破旧外套上，另一块破布盖着工作台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than turn the shabby trousers into a rag, he added a patch and loosened the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tight</strong></a> waistband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他没有把破旧裤子改成抹布，而是补上补丁、放松了紧腰头。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tight fit</span> · 紧身的尺寸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修补</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wear%20and%20tear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wear and tear</a></span> · 磨损</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 11.08｜颜色、褪色与身形 {#group-11-08}

**串联方式：** 先辨颜色和晒成棕褐色，再看褪色、污渍，最后描述整体身形。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-100">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-100" class="vocabulary-word-title">colour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-100" aria-label="定位 colour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-100" data-mark-label="colour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="colour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="colour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 colour" title="在有道查询 colour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-100</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(=color)；n.颜色颜料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colour</strong></a> chart shows white and grey neutrals beside yellow and brown.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">色卡把白色、灰色中性色放在黄色和棕色旁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A white shirt, yellow scarf and brown belt add <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colour</strong></a> to grey trousers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">白衬衫、黄围巾和棕腰带给灰裤子增添色彩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose grey or white as the main <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colour</strong></a>, then add yellow stitching and brown buttons.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选灰或白作主色，再加黄色缝线和棕色纽扣。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a neutral colour</span> · 中性色</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=palette&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">palette</a></span> · 配色方案</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contrast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contrast</a></span> · 对比</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose grey or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=white&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>white</strong></a> as the main colour, then add yellow stitching and brown buttons.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选灰或白作主色，再加黄色缝线和棕色纽扣。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pink</strong></a> and purple fabrics may fade faster than the tan leather.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">粉色和紫色布料可能比棕黄色皮革褪色更快。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pink</strong></a> dress, purple scarf and tan bag away from strong sunlight so they do not fade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把粉裙、紫围巾和棕黄色包远离强光，以免褪色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After years in the window, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pink</strong></a>, purple and tan samples began to fade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">放在窗边多年后，粉色、紫色和棕黄色样品开始褪色。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">stain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-109" aria-label="定位 stain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-109" data-mark-label="stain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stain" title="在有道查询 stain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.染污；n.污渍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Blot the ink carefully before it can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stain</strong></a> the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">墨水染污布料前，先小心吸干。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A dark blot on the sleeve became a permanent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stain</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">袖子上的一块深色污点变成了永久污渍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not rub the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stain</strong></a>; blot it gently with a clean cloth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要揉搓污渍；用干净布轻轻吸干。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">remove a stain</span> · 去污渍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorbent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">absorbent</a></span> · 吸水的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spot%20cleaning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spot cleaning</a></span> · 局部清洁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="11-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-11-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">blot<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-11-110" aria-label="定位 blot">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="11-110" data-mark-label="blot" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="blot：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="blot：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 blot" title="在有道查询 blot">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">11-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.污点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Blot</strong></a> the ink carefully before it can stain the fabric.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">墨水染污布料前，先小心吸干。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A dark <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blot</strong></a> on the sleeve became a permanent stain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">袖子上的一块深色污点变成了永久污渍。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not rub the stain; <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blot</strong></a> it gently with a clean cloth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要揉搓污渍；用干净布轻轻吸干。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an ink blot</span> · 墨渍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorbent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">absorbent</a></span> · 吸水的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spot%20cleaning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spot cleaning</a></span> · 局部清洁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **follow a fashion trend**：追随时尚潮流；follow 在第 20 章出现。
- **a wool sweater**：羊毛毛衣。
- **a decent appearance**：得体的外表。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

Fashion trends influence the clothes and accessories people choose. A jacket may be made of wool, while a scarf can add colour to an outfit. Some people prefer an elegant appearance, and others value comfort. Looking at fabric, stitching and fit helps us describe clothes more precisely.

**中文理解：** 时尚潮流影响人们选择的衣服和配件。夹克可以用羊毛制作，围巾可以为整套穿搭增加色彩。有些人喜欢优雅的外表，另一些人重视舒适。观察面料、缝线和合身程度，可以让服装描述更准确。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 描述一套穿搭：风格、衣物、面料、颜色、配件和穿着状态。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
